#### Abstracts
##### **Towards Trustworthy Autonomous Robots: An Explainable AI-Based Decision Framework**
2609.02861v1 by Cagri Temel

Autonomous robots powered by deep learning face a fundamental auditability challenge: when incidents occur, investigators cannot reconstruct why the system made specific decisions. This paper presents TRACE (Transparent Reasoning Architecture for Credible Execution), a decision framework that ensures every autonomous action can be traced back to sensor evidence through documented causal chains. The framework organizes decision-making into four auditable layers: Semantic Perception for evidence-grounded entity recognition, Belief Reasoning for probabilistic state estimation with causal graphs, Action Synthesis for constraint-aware planning with counterfactual documentation, and Execution Verification for compliance monitoring. TRACE is model-agnostic yet designed to integrate learning-based perception modules (CNNs, transformers) while preserving decision-level auditability. We evaluate the framework using three objective metrics: Evidence Traceability (sensor-to-decision linkage), Decision Reconstructability (post-hoc analysis capability), and Temporal Continuity (audit trail completeness). Experimental evaluation on warehouse robot navigation demonstrates that TRACE achieves 98.6% evidence traceability, 99.0% temporal continuity, and 98.1% decision reconstructability across 500 simulated decision cycles. Post-hoc methods like LIME provide feature attributions but lack the artifact structure needed for decision-level reconstruction. The framework addresses EU AI Act requirements for high-risk system transparency and contributes to Explainable AI for safety-critical autonomous systems.

##### **Cheap Verifiers, Large Blind Spots: Measuring the Reliability Cost of Cost-Saving Cascades**

##### **Life Operators: a self-evolving framework for multiscale life modelling**
2609.00068v1 by Shuo Wang, Yike Guo

Medical AI is moving beyond recognition towards clinical dialogue and longitudinal prediction. Yet a central question remains: how would a patient's state change under intervention? Statistical models learn future observations, whereas mechanistic models describe selected processes. Neither provides a common framework for representing patient state, coupling scales or revising failed assumptions. We propose Life Operators: task-bounded mappings that define three scientific roles. Perception operators infer task-relevant biological states from multimodal observations, Evolution operators propagate these states under natural or intervention-conditioned dynamics, and Generation operators map them to measurable signals. Each role may be realised by equations, statistical models, neural networks or hybrids. Bridge operators connect components with different variables, scales and time steps. Selected operators and bridges form task-specific Operator Graphs containing the smallest set of states and mechanisms sufficient for a declared claim. This modular structure also makes scientific revision localisable. An AI co-scientist may propose changes to states, operators, bridges or graph structure, while independent evidence determines which variants are retained, restricted or retired. 摘要：視覺-語言模型（VLMs）在視頻和圖像序列基準上表現出色，但仍不清楚它們是否捕捉到時間結構。為了研究這個問題，我們將時間基準制定為異常檢測問題，提供一個簡單且可控的評估，直接測試對時間一致性的敏感性。我們引入了TimeCatch，其中通過交換連續幀來創建時間異常，並通過用高斯噪聲替換幀來創建幀級異常。模型在四個合成和現實世界數據集上進行異常檢測和定位任務的評估，並進行了一項人類研究。我們的評估揭示了幀級異常檢測和時間異常檢測之間的顯著差距。雖然VLMs始終能夠檢測幀級異常並且經常準確定位它們，但在時間異常檢測上表現接近隨機，並且在定位上僅略高於隨機。相比之下，人類在這兩項任務上達到了接近上限的表現。對模型規模、提示策略、序列長度和視覺相似性的額外分析表明，這些失敗不能僅僅通過感知或模型能力的限制來解釋。綜合這些發現表明，當前的VLMs可以識別單個幀內的異常，但在整合跨幀的信息以推理時間一致性方面存在困難。TimeCatch提供了一個可控的基準，用於評估視覺-語言模型中的時間基準。

##### **Characterizing Necessary Losers to Explain Tournaments Solutions**
2608.23446v2 by Contet Clément, Umberto Grandi, Jérôme Mengin

We study the problem of formally explaining why a candidate was not selected by a given tournament rule, by identifying sub-tournaments in which the candidate loses independently of how the rest of the tournament is completed. We define destructive minimal supports as any minimal sub-tournament satisfying this property, which in formal explainable artificial intelligence corresponds to abductive explanations for the question "Why does the loser lose the tournament?". For six common tournament solutions (maximin, uncovered set and its weighted variant, top cycle, Copeland, and Borda) we provide characterizations of when a candidate is either a necessary loser or a possible winner, we determine the size of the smallest destructive minimal supports, complemented by polynomial-time algorithms for their computation except for the case of Borda and Copeland rules which we conjecture to also be polynomial.

摘要：我們研究了正式解釋為什麼候選人未被特定比賽規則選中這個問題，通過識別候選人在子比賽中輸的情況，無論比賽的其餘部分如何完成。<lb/>我們將破壞性最小支持定義為滿足這一特性的任何最小子比賽，這在正式可解釋的人工智慧中對應於對「為什麼輸家會輸掉比賽？」這個問題的推理解釋。<lb/>對於六種常見的比賽解決方案（最大最小、未覆蓋集及其加權變體、頂部循環、科佩蘭和博爾達），我們提供了候選人是必要輸家或可能贏家的情況的特徵描述，並確定了最小破壞性支持的大小，除了博爾達和科佩蘭規則的情況外，我們還提供了多項式時間算法來計算它們，對於這兩種規則，我們推測它們也應該是多項式的。

##### **Hidden in the Request: Explaining Unethical LLM Compliance through Token Relevance**
2608.23264v1 by Or Biton, Tomer Krichli, Itai Allouche, Joseph Keshet

Although Large Language Models (LLMs) are aligned to optimize for both helpfulness and harmlessness, these dual objectives may conflict, inevitably leading to alignment failures. This work systematically investigates instances where LLMs fail to exhibit ethical behavior. To understand the underlying mechanics of these vulnerabilities, we introduce a probing methodology that presents unethical scenarios to LLMs in three distinct structural modalities: objective classification tasks, subjective first-person statements, and direct requests for assistance. We find that model performance degrades in the request-for-assistance-based form. Using Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP), we trace this discrepancy to an attribution bias: the model places greater emphasis on benign task-framing tokens (e.g., "Can you help me...") than on tokens signaling the underlying unethical behavior (e.g., "without getting caught"), which we term cue-tokens. We hypothesize that this under-attribution contributes to harmful compliance. To test this, we introduce two LRP-guided decoding methods that steer generation toward trajectories more relevant to cue tokens. Empirical evaluations show that these interventions promote safer responses, supporting cue-token attribution's role in compliance failures.

摘要：雖然大型語言模型（LLMs）旨在優化有用性和無害性，但這兩個目標可能會發生衝突，最終導致對齊失敗。這項工作系統性地調查了LLMs未能展現倫理行為的情況。為了了解這些脆弱性的潛在機制，我們引入了一種探測方法，向LLMs呈現三種不同結構模式的不道德場景：客觀分類任務、主觀第一人稱陳述和直接請求協助。我們發現，在基於請求協助的形式中，模型性能下降。使用逐層相關性傳播（LRP），我們追蹤這一差異至一種歸因偏見：模型對良性任務框架標記（例如，“你能幫我...嗎？”）的重視程度高於對標記潛在不道德行為的標記（例如，“不被抓到”），我們稱之為提示標記。我們假設這種低估歸因促進了有害的遵從性。為了測試這一點，我們引入了兩種LRP引導的解碼方法，將生成引導至更相關於提示標記的軌跡。實證評估顯示，這些干預促進了更安全的回應，支持了提示標記歸因在遵從失敗中的作用。

##### **Counterfactual Transition Graphs: Evaluating Cross-Class Transition Quality**
2608.23164v1 by Syed Muhammad Hamza Zaidi, Szymon Bobek, Grzegorz J. Nalepa, Myra Spiliopoulou

Counterfactual (CF) explanations for time-series classifiers are usually evaluated one example at a time: what minimal edit flips this single window's prediction? We argue that the more informative question for diagnostic interpretability is structural: how does the classifier connect its own classes to each other?
  We propose a counterfactual transition graph (CGT) in which each node is a class and each edge weight is the CF reliability of the transition from one prototype to another under a proximity aware retrieval sweep. On a six-class hand-movement task, we induce a CGT that reveals a non-trivial topology, which is not predicted by the binary confusion matrix: it shows that counterfactual reachability does not align with classifier accuracy and even runs counter to it (Spearman $ρ=-0.37$ over the 15 pairs), i.e. the boundaries the classifier separates most confidently are among those an in-distribution edit can least often cross.
  Our framework is method agnostic, i.e. any CF-explainers can be used. Presently, we use it to juxtapose replacement-based CFs with gradient-based CFs; gradient-based methods reach almost any class by stepping off the data manifold, while replacement-based methods stay on it and fail on precisely the rigid boundaries.

摘要：反事實（CF）解釋時間序列分類器通常是逐個例子進行評估：什麼最小的編輯可以翻轉這個單一窗口的預測？我們認為，對於診斷可解釋性來說，更具信息性的問題是結構性的：分類器是如何將自身的類別彼此連接的？<lb/>  
我們提出了一個反事實轉換圖（CGT），其中每個節點代表一個類別，每條邊的權重是從一個原型到另一個原型的CF可靠性，這是在考慮接近度的檢索掃描下進行的。在一個六類手部動作任務中，我們引導出一個CGT，揭示了一個非平凡的拓撲，這並不是由二元混淆矩陣所預測的：它顯示反事實可達性與分類器的準確性並不一致，甚至與之相悖（Spearman $ρ=-0.37$ 在15對中），即分類器最自信地分隔的邊界恰恰是那些在分佈內編輯中最少能夠跨越的。<lb/>  
我們的框架是方法無關的，即可以使用任何CF解釋器。目前，我們用它來並列基於替換的CF與基於梯度的CF；基於梯度的方法幾乎可以通過偏離數據流形來達到任何類別，而基於替換的方法則保持在流形上，並在精確的剛性邊界上失敗。

##### **PsychJail: Exploring Psychological Jailbreaks via Multi-Turn Persuasion of LLM Policies**
2608.23028v1 by Zeyu Feng, Qingyu Wu, Yuzhe Luo, Hua Cheng

Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in education, healthcare, policy advising, and other interactive settings, where users engage them as sustained social interlocutors rather than one-shot query engines. This shift makes jailbreaks a growing safety threat, yet most research emphasizes single-turn prompt optimization or iterative attack refinement, leaving psychologically grounded multi-turn vulnerabilities underexplored. We present PsychJail, a psychology-guided framework for red teaming aligned LLMs through theory-grounded, multi-turn persuasion. PsychJail maps established social-psychological persuasion techniques into a tactic-conditioned attack policy. It factorizes each attacker action into a Change-of-Meaning analysis, tactic selection, and victim-visible message, operationalizing the Persuasion Knowledge Model (PKM). The policy is refined with trajectory-level reinforcement learning using a PKM-gated reward that credits early jailbreak success only when every turn contains a well-formed Change-of-Meaning analysis. Across four aligned victim models, PsychJail achieves the highest average attack success rate (87.3%) and outperforms strong single-turn and multi-turn baselines on every model. We also measure susceptibility at the action that breaks each victim, revealing four distinct model-level fingerprints that identify which persuasion levers affect each model and how broadly. These fingerprints help explain cross-model transfer asymmetry. We interpret them as four candidate psychological profiles-rationalist, credibility-driven, narrative-monoculture, and broadly persuadable-while treating this interpretation as a conjecture requiring future validation. Our findings establish psychological jailbreaks as a distinct red-teaming frontier for increasingly interactive LLMs.

摘要：大型語言模型（LLMs）越來越多地應用於教育、醫療、政策諮詢以及其他互動環境中，使用者將其視為持續的社交對話者，而非一次性查詢引擎。這一轉變使得越獄成為日益增長的安全威脅，但大多數研究強調單回合提示優化或迭代攻擊精煉，未能充分探討心理學基礎的多回合脆弱性。我們提出了PsychJail，一個以心理學為指導的框架，通過理論基礎的多回合說服來進行紅隊測試，對齊LLMs。PsychJail將已建立的社會心理學說服技術映射到戰術條件攻擊政策中。它將每個攻擊者行動分解為意義變更分析、戰術選擇和受害者可見消息，將說服知識模型（PKM）具體化。該政策通過使用PKM閘控獎勵的軌跡級強化學習進行精煉，只有當每一回合都包含良好形成的意義變更分析時，才會對早期越獄成功給予獎勵。在四個對齊的受害者模型中，PsychJail實現了最高的平均攻擊成功率（87.3%），並在每個模型上超越了強大的單回合和多回合基準。我們還測量了每個受害者被攻破時的脆弱性，揭示了四個不同的模型級指紋，這些指紋識別出哪些說服槓桿影響每個模型以及影響的廣泛程度。這些指紋有助於解釋跨模型轉移的不對稱性。我們將其解釋為四種候選心理特徵——理性主義者、以可信度驅動者、敘事單一文化和廣泛可說服者——同時將此解釋視為需要未來驗證的推測。我們的研究結果確立了心理學越獄作為對日益互動的LLMs的一個獨特紅隊測試前沿。

##### **A Physical Response-and-Memory Model for Muon Optimization**
2608.22994v1 by Yinze Hu, Hongjun Xiang, Xingao Gong, Hongyu Yu

Training large language models is costly. How low a loss the same compute can ultimately reach depends on how each step's gradient is converted into a weight update; the rule that performs this conversion is the optimizer. From SGD and AdamW to the recent Muon, effective update rules have mostly been shaped by engineering intuition and then selected on benchmarks. Muon semi-orthogonalizes the momentum matrix before applying the update and has kept breaking records on public training benchmarks; yet why the semi-orthogonalized direction works, and over how long a history the momentum should average, are two questions at present answered mainly by experience. Here we treat the weight matrix during training as a responsive medium with memory and build a physical model for it, in which both questions find answers: the semi-orthogonalized direction is the maximally dissipative response under an output-side safety budget, which explains why it works; momentum is the internal stress accumulated by the medium; how long it should average is set by the relaxation of this stress, and a real medium relaxes on more than one timescale, the simplest form being one fast and one slow. On this basis we propose the Bi-Maxwell optimizer. The framework further yields a testable consequence: gradient directions change fast early in training and more slowly later, so the optimal memory length should grow with training stage; step-by-step measurements of a proxy for it by a read-only probe across 8 independent training trajectories are consistent with this consequence. Replacing the memory kernel alone, from a single timescale to two, brings training to the target loss in noticeably fewer steps on a public large-language-model optimizer benchmark.

摘要：訓練大型語言模型的成本很高。相同計算能達到的損失低度最終取決於每一步的梯度如何轉換為權重更新；執行此轉換的規則是優化器。從SGD和AdamW到最近的Muon，有效的更新規則大多是基於工程直覺形成的，然後在基準測試中進行選擇。Muon在應用更新之前對動量矩陣進行半正交化，並在公共訓練基準上不斷打破紀錄；然而，為什麼半正交化的方向有效，以及動量應該平均多長時間，這兩個問題目前主要依賴經驗來回答。在這裡，我們將訓練過程中的權重矩陣視為一種具有記憶的響應介質，並為其建立一個物理模型，在這個模型中，兩個問題都找到了答案：半正交化的方向是在輸出端安全預算下的最大耗散響應，這解釋了它為什麼有效；動量是介質所累積的內部應力；應該平均多長時間由這種應力的放鬆來決定，而一個真實的介質在不止一個時間尺度上放鬆，最簡單的形式是一個快的和一個慢的。在此基礎上，我們提出了Bi-Maxwell優化器。該框架進一步產生了一個可測試的結果：在訓練初期，梯度方向變化迅速，而在後期變化較慢，因此最佳記憶長度應隨著訓練階段增長；通過一個只讀探針對8條獨立訓練軌跡進行的逐步測量與這一結果是一致的。僅僅將記憶核從單一時間尺度替換為兩個，便使訓練在公共大型語言模型優化器基準上以明顯更少的步驟達到目標損失。

#### Abstracts
##### **Untangling the Mechanisms of Misleading Context in Medical Question Answering**
2609.02754v1 by Robin Linzmayer, Noémie Elhadad

Large language models now answer medical questions with expert-level performance. However, the context these systems act on can be misleading, and misleading context can corrupt a model's medical judgment. To understand how misleading context corrupts this judgment, we examine the model's susceptibility to the context, disclosure of it, mechanism of corrupted reasoning, and monitorability of the decision. On the medical reasoning subset of MedMisBench, a clinician-reviewed question-answering benchmark of 8,627 questions, we inject two types of misleading context cues, fabricated evidence and a bare assertion. We test three reasoning models, two that expose their full reasoning trace and one frontier model that exposes only its response. All three are more susceptible to the assertion than to the fabricated evidence, adopting the asserted answer 10 to 27 points more often. The misleading cues are disclosed in 81 to 98% of traces but only 7 to 90% of responses, and the assertion is disclosed less often than evidence based cues. Resampling from reasoning traces without disclosure shows the two cues corrupt reasoning differently, evidence entering early and accumulating while the assertion redirects the conclusion near its end. An LLM monitor catches 78% of corrupted decisions at 5% false positives when reading an open model's trace with guidance, against at most 32% from any response. The misleading context that models are most susceptible to is disclosed least, and was caught reliably only from an open reasoning trace, which frontier providers withhold.

摘要：大型語言模型現在能以專家級的表現回答醫療問題。<lb/>然而，這些系統所依賴的背景可能會產生誤導，而誤導性的背景可能會損害模型的醫療判斷。<lb/>為了理解誤導性背景如何損害這種判斷，我們檢視模型對背景的敏感性、其披露情況、損壞推理的機制以及決策的可監控性。<lb/>在 MedMisBench 的醫療推理子集上，這是一個由臨床醫生審核的問題回答基準，共有 8,627 個問題，我們注入了兩種誤導性背景提示，虛構證據和單純主張。<lb/>我們測試了三種推理模型，其中兩種展示其完整的推理過程，另一種前沿模型則僅展示其回應。<lb/>這三種模型對於主張的敏感性高於虛構證據，接受主張的答案的頻率高出 10 到 27 個百分點。<lb/>誤導性提示在 81% 到 98% 的推理過程中被披露，但在 7% 到 90% 的回應中僅披露少數，且主張的披露頻率低於基於證據的提示。<lb/>從未披露的推理過程中重新抽樣顯示，這兩種提示對推理的損害方式不同，證據早期進入並累積，而主張則在結論接近結尾時重新導向。<lb/>一個 LLM 監控器在閱讀開放模型的推理過程時，能以 5% 的假陽性率捕捉到 78% 的損壞決策，而從任何回應中最多僅能捕捉到 32%。<lb/>模型最容易受到的誤導性背景披露最少，且僅從開放推理過程中可靠捕捉到，而前沿提供者則不予披露。

##### **HiPoly: a hierarchical polymer-native AI framework for property prediction and generative design**
2609.02746v1 by Ge Sun, Gervasio Zaldivar, Yuan Tian, Gustavo Perez Lemus, Juhae Park, Dasha Safarian, Ming Han, Juan J. de Pablo

Polymeric materials are central to modern technologies, with applications ranging from energy to health and transportation. Although AI has made significant advances in materials discovery, the hierarchical structure of polymers across multiple length scales makes them inherently difficult to represent in a unified and physically meaningful way. Here we introduce HiPoly, a polymer-native AI framework that processes complete polymer descriptions through a three-level hierarchical graph architecture built on the G2RINS representation. HiPoly encodes stochastic inter-monomer connectivity, composition, and molecular weight directly within its architecture, using physically motivated design principles that mirror the multi-scale nature of polymeric systems. The framework establishes an end-to-end AI-driven workflow from experimental formulation data to property prediction, generative molecular design, and physics-based validation through molecular simulations, all unified by a single polymer representation. We demonstrate state-of-the-art prediction accuracy for thermophysical properties of multi-component polymer systems, with ablation studies confirming that each hierarchical design choice contributes independently to model performance. As an example, the generative design pathway is applied here to the discovery of sustainable alternatives to persistent fluorinated polymers, where it is possible to identify and independently validate PFAS-free candidates with target surface-energy properties. This work demonstrates how polymer-native AI can accelerate discovery by linking representation, prediction, and design across complex polymer chemistries.

摘要：聚合材料是現代技術的核心，應用範圍從能源到健康和交通。儘管人工智慧在材料發現方面取得了重大進展，但聚合物在多個長度尺度上的層次結構使其本質上難以以統一且物理上有意義的方式進行表示。在此，我們介紹 HiPoly，一個聚合物原生的人工智慧框架，通過基於 G2RINS 表示的三層層次圖架構處理完整的聚合物描述。HiPoly 在其架構中直接編碼隨機的單體間連接性、組成和分子量，使用反映聚合物系統多尺度特性的物理驅動設計原則。該框架建立了一個端到端的人工智慧驅動工作流程，從實驗配方數據到性質預測、生成分子設計及通過分子模擬的基於物理的驗證，所有這些都由單一的聚合物表示統一。我們展示了對多組分聚合物系統的熱物理性質的最先進預測準確性，消融研究確認每個層次設計選擇獨立地對模型性能作出貢獻。作為一個例子，這裡應用生成設計途徑來發現持久氟化聚合物的可持續替代品，在這裡可以識別並獨立驗證具有目標表面能特性的無 PFAS 候選者。這項工作展示了聚合物原生人工智慧如何通過連結表示、預測和設計來加速發現，跨越複雜的聚合物化學。

##### **From Tokens to Semantics: Leveraging Complementary Signals for Hallucination Detection in Black-Box LLMs**
2609.02679v1 by Urja Pawar, Rajitha Ramanayake, Owen O'Neill, Nabeel Kemal, Abhishek Mandal, Houssem Chatbri, Christopher Martin

When LLMs support public-facing or high-stakes workflows, missed fabrications can harm users and institutions, while false alarms consume limited human-review capacity. When no trusted context or reference document is available, we study two signals accessible through black-box model APIs: semantic entropy, which measures disagreement among sampled response meanings, and uncertainty derived from token log-probabilities. Their failure modes can be complementary: semantic entropy becomes uninformative when responses form one semantic cluster, while token uncertainty can miss consistently confident errors. We extend token-based uncertainty detection by aggregating token-level signals across sampled responses through our TopK method, evaluate the hybrid CoCoA method, which combines target-response uncertainty with semantic dissimilarity, and propose and study two supervised methods: Gated, which routes single-cluster cases to an aggregated-token-feature classifier, and Stacked, which learns jointly from semantic uncertainty and broader token features. We evaluate seven benchmarks, including five public benchmarks (four text datasets and multimodal handwritten-cheque extraction) and two constructed benchmarks (Financial Summaries and Long-Text QA), using four language models. In our evaluation across models and datasets, Stacked gave the best performance in nearly half of the cases, while TopK and CoCoA remain competitive without supervised training labels, although their thresholds require careful calibration. No method is universally strongest. We therefore evaluate performance at false-positive-rate budgets from 1% to 15%, assess their sensitivity to generation and calibration choices, and examine variation across dataset characteristics.

摘要：當大型語言模型（LLMs）支持面向公眾或高風險的工作流程時，錯過的虛構內容可能會對用戶和機構造成傷害，而虛假警報則會消耗有限的人類審查能力。當沒有可信的背景或參考文件可用時，我們研究了通過黑箱模型API可獲得的兩種信號：語義熵，它測量抽樣回應意義之間的不一致性，以及源自標記對數概率的不確定性。它們的失效模式可能是互補的：當回應形成一個語義集群時，語義熵變得沒有信息，而標記不確定性則可能錯過持續自信的錯誤。我們通過我們的TopK方法擴展了基於標記的不確定性檢測，通過聚合抽樣回應中的標記級信號，評估混合的CoCoA方法，該方法將目標回應不確定性與語義差異性結合，並提出並研究兩種監督方法：Gated，將單一集群案例路由到聚合標記特徵分類器，以及Stacked，從語義不確定性和更廣泛的標記特徵中共同學習。我們使用四種語言模型評估七個基準，包括五個公共基準（四個文本數據集和多模態手寫支票提取）和兩個構建的基準（財務摘要和長文本問答）。在我們對模型和數據集的評估中，Stacked在近一半的情況下表現最佳，而TopK和CoCoA在沒有監督訓練標籤的情況下仍然具有競爭力，儘管它們的閾值需要仔細校準。沒有任何方法是普遍最強的。因此，我們在1%到15%的假陽性率預算下評估性能，評估它們對生成和校準選擇的敏感性，並檢查數據集特徵之間的變化。

##### **ORB-SVM : An Innovative Hybrid Framework for Efficient Brain Tumor Detection from MRI Scans**
2609.02333v1 by Amirhosein Azarpour

Brain cancer remains one of the most significant challenges in modern medicine, where the accuracy of early stage diagnosis is a decisive factor in patient survival and treatment efficacy. Although Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is the established gold standard for visualizing neurological structures, the interpretation of these high dimensional scans is often complicated by subjective variability among practitioners and the inherent noise present in complex medical images. While contemporary approaches frequently rely on high parameter deep learning architectures, such models often involve significant computational costs and require extensive data for effective training. This study introduces a hybrid framework that utilizes the Oriented FAST and Rotated BRIEF (ORB) algorithm for precise feature extraction and a Support Vector Machine (SVM) for classification [1], [2]. The proposed approach achieves a sub- stantial data reduction of approximately 99.5%, which effectively minimizes the influence of non informative background data while preserving critical diagnostic patterns essential for tumor identification. By balancing feature sparsity with a robust kernel based classifier, this methodology addresses the limitations of over parameterized systems while maintaining high diagnostic integrity. Medical AI is moving beyond recognition towards clinical dialogue and longitudinal prediction. Yet a central question remains: how would a patient's state change under intervention? ##### **EviDx: Evidence-Aware Active Diagnosis with Scaffolded LLM Agents**

Agentic LLMs offer a dynamic alternative through tool use and intermediate diagnostic trajectories, but existing systems often under-specify how patient evidence should be exposed, scaffolded, and controlled at runtime. #### Abstracts
##### **Towards Trustworthy Autonomous Robots: An Explainable AI-Based Decision Framework**
2609.02861v1 by Cagri Temel

Autonomous robots powered by deep learning face a fundamental auditability challenge: when incidents occur, investigators cannot reconstruct why the system made specific decisions. This paper presents TRACE (Transparent Reasoning Architecture for Credible Execution), a decision framework that ensures every autonomous action can be traced back to sensor evidence through documented causal chains. The framework organizes decision-making into four auditable layers: Semantic Perception for evidence-grounded entity recognition, Belief Reasoning for probabilistic state estimation with causal graphs, Action Synthesis for constraint-aware planning with counterfactual documentation, and Execution Verification for compliance monitoring. TRACE is model-agnostic yet designed to integrate learning-based perception modules (CNNs, transformers) while preserving decision-level auditability. We evaluate the framework using three objective metrics: Evidence Traceability (sensor-to-decision linkage), Decision Reconstructability (post-hoc analysis capability), and Temporal Continuity (audit trail completeness). Experimental evaluation on warehouse robot navigation demonstrates that TRACE achieves 98.6% evidence traceability, 99.0% temporal continuity, and 98.1% decision reconstructability across 500 simulated decision cycles. Post-hoc methods like LIME provide feature attributions but lack the artifact structure needed for decision-level reconstruction. The framework addresses EU AI Act requirements for high-risk system transparency and contributes to Explainable AI for safety-critical autonomous systems.

摘要：自主機器人由深度學習驅動，面臨一個基本的可審計性挑戰：當事件發生時，調查人員無法重建系統為何做出特定決策的原因。本文提出TRACE（透明推理架構以確保可信執行），這是一個決策框架，確保每一個自主行動都能通過記錄的因果鏈追溯到感測器證據。該框架將決策過程組織為四個可審計的層級：語義感知，用於基於證據的實體識別；信念推理，利用因果圖進行概率狀態估計；行動合成，進行考慮約束的規劃並附有反事實文檔；執行驗證，用於合規監控。TRACE是模型無關的，但設計上能夠整合基於學習的感知模組（CNNs，Transformer），同時保持決策層級的可審計性。我們使用三個客觀指標來評估該框架：證據可追溯性（感測器與決策的連結）、決策可重建性（事後分析能力）和時間連續性（審計追蹤的完整性）。在倉庫機器人導航的實驗評估中，TRACE在500個模擬決策循環中實現了98.6%的證據可追溯性、99.0%的時間連續性和98.1%的決策可重建性。事後方法如LIME提供特徵歸因，但缺乏決策層級重建所需的工件結構。該框架滿足歐盟人工智慧法案對高風險系統透明度的要求，並對安全關鍵的自主系統的可解釋人工智慧作出貢獻。

##### **Post-Training Language Models for Gold-Medal Performance in Coding Competitions**
2609.02849v1 by Aleksander Ficek, Sean Narenthiran, Mehrzad Samadi, Somshubra Majumdar, Boris Ginsburg

Competitive programming has become a key test of large language model reasoning, with international competitions such as IOI and ICPC representing its most challenging settings. We present an end-to-end specialization pipeline combining large-scale problem curation, synthetic reasoning traces, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL). Using 22,000 curated problems, we train Nemotron-3-Nano-CC (30B-A3B) with SFT and RL and Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC (550B-A55B) with SFT alone. We further introduce GenCorrect, a feedback-driven test-time compute strategy that iteratively generates, evaluates, and refines diverse solutions. On IOI 2025, Nano-CC improves from 130 points to 291 after post-training and to 468 with GenCorrect, exceeding the gold threshold of 438.3 while Ultra-CC reaches 502. Guided by these results, we develop a competition-specific Ultra-CC system and evaluate it prospectively during IOI 2026. Under the same time, internet-access, and submission constraints as human contestants, it scores 535.4 out of 600, exceeding both the gold threshold of 361.12 and the top human score of 498.27. To our knowledge, this is the first AI system to outscore the highest-scoring human contestant on an IOI problem set.

摘要：競賽編程已成為大型語言模型推理的一項關鍵測試，國際競賽如 IOI 和 ICPC 代表了其最具挑戰性的環境。<lb/>我們提出了一個端到端的專門化管道，結合了大規模問題策劃、合成推理痕跡、監督微調（SFT）和強化學習（RL）。<lb/>使用 22,000 個策劃問題，我們用 SFT 和 RL 訓練了 Nemotron-3-Nano-CC（30B-A3B），並僅用 SFT 訓練了 Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC（550B-A55B）。<lb/>我們進一步介紹了 GenCorrect，一種基於反饋的測試時間計算策略，通過迭代生成、評估和改進多樣化解決方案。<lb/>在 IOI 2025 中，Nano-CC 在後訓練後從 130 分提高到 291，並在使用 GenCorrect 後達到 468，超過了 438.3 的金牌門檻，而 Ultra-CC 則達到了 502。<lb/>根據這些結果，我們開發了一個特定於競賽的 Ultra-CC 系統，並在 IOI 2026 中進行了前瞻性評估。<lb/>在與人類參賽者相同的時間、網絡訪問和提交限制下，它的得分為 535.4 分（滿分 600），超過了 361.12 的金牌門檻和 498.27 的最高人類得分。<lb/>據我們所知，這是第一個在 IOI 問題集上超越最高得分人類參賽者的 AI 系統。

##### **Large Language Models (LLMs) for Telecom Root Cause Analysis (RCA): A Structured Reasoning Framework for Evidence-Grounded Diagnosis**
2609.02805v1 by Hao Zhou, Mandar Kulkarni, Hao Chen, Yan Xin,  Charlie,  Zhang

Root cause analysis (RCA) is a critical task in telecom network operations, but diagnosing performance degradations in modern 5G and emerging 6G networks remains challenging due to complex cross-layer dependencies. While large language models (LLMs) offer promising capabilities for reasoning and knowledge integration, directly applying vanilla LLMs to telecom RCA often leads to hallucination, unstable reasoning, and poor alignment with structured network evidence. This work first reviews the evolution of telecom RCA from rule-based and machine learning (ML) approaches to emerging LLM-enabled techniques, and provides an overview of recent paradigms, including structured reasoning, retrieval-augmented knowledge grounding, agentic orchestration, and verifiable reasoning. Building upon these insights, we propose a structured reasoning framework for LLM-enabled telecom RCA that aligns diagnostic reasoning with telecom-specific evidence and domain knowledge. The proposed approach first organizes heterogeneous network telemetry into canonical contexts, and then enforces decision-path reasoning during diagnosis, and finally generates evidence-grounded explanations for reliable fault identification. Experimental results on two 5G RCA datasets, TeleLogs and TelecomTS, demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves diagnostic accuracy and decision consistency compared with baseline techniques. These cross-dataset results highlight the importance of structured reasoning design for practical LLM-based RCA systems in next-generation telecom networks.

##### **A Power Law in Logarithm's Clothing: On the Scalability of Graph-Based Vector Search**

Most vector databases rely on graph-based indexes, notably HNSW and Vamana, for approximate nearest neighbor search. With embedding models widely adopted, the datasets these databases store grow rapidly. At a fixed accuracy, how does search cost scale with dataset size? The prevailing answer is poly-logarithmic growth. Yet the claim is proven only under special conditions and asserted without proof for the indexes used in practice. It is also largely untested: standard benchmarks measure cost at one dataset size, not across sizes. We put the claim to the test. The answer depends on the scale itself. While the dataset size $N$ is small relative to the data's intrinsic dimensionality, search cost grows as $N^c$ for a constant $0<c<1$. We call this scaling the Sublinear Power Law. Once $N$ is large enough, growth slows to subpolynomial, consistent with the poly-logarithmic claim. The Sublinear Power Law appears on every dataset, mostly up to its full size, at every recall target, query hardness level, and index configuration we test. The transition to subpolynomial growth appears on the two datasets that grow large enough relative to their intrinsic dimensionality. One mechanism underlies both behaviors: a dataset's intrinsic dimensionality grows with its size until the data resolves its underlying distribution. Higher intrinsic dimensionality packs more vectors into the query neighborhood the search must examine. We present a unifying theory of beam-search cost that explains our observations. For exact and bounded-degree constructions, we prove the Sublinear Power Law and the eventual transition to poly-logarithmic scaling, and derive the scale at which it occurs. We also develop models that predict the power-law exponents for any recall target and index configuration. 摘要：有價值的數據仍然嵌入在非結構化的來源中：網頁、報告、合同、申報、財報電話會議和PDF檔案。企業AI的重大賭注是部署LLM代理，讓它們能夠對這些數據進行推理，以回答每位知識工作者的複雜問題。代理今天可以做到這一點，但成本卻非常高昂。每個問題都會重複打開大型文件以恢復分散的證據，消耗多達一百萬個標記。然而，如果數據已經結構化，則相同的問題將簡化為便宜的數據庫查詢。例如，在FanOutQA基準上，對理想的預結構化存儲進行推理的成本便宜28倍，隨著問題在更多文件中擴展，這一差距會增長到幾個數量級。然而，事先結構化所有內容並不可行：文件中潛在的結構遠遠超過任何工作負載所需的結構，而有用的結構和文件在查詢到達之前是未知的。我們提出了代理數據破解，這是一種在推理過程中自適應和推測性地結構化非結構化數據的方法。結構化是自適應的，因為觀察到的查詢決定了何時發生以及什麼是重要的，並且是推測性的，因為它超越了當前的問題。每當代理打開一個文件以回答問題時，一個破解子代理會以邊際成本從已加載的上下文中分叉出來，並提取可能服務於相關未來查詢的基礎結構。隨著時間的推移，越來越多的查詢完全由結構化數據覆蓋，並且在不打開文件的情況下得到回答，保持代理的準確性接近RAG成本。在FanOutQA上，僅在每個測試問題中擴展一個相關問題，破解成本降低53%，同時保持準確性。代理數據破解是朝著下一代數據基礎設施邁出的第一步，旨在實現對非結構化數據的代理推理：模型下方的共享基質，知識在推理過程中已經付出代價以揭示並積累。

#### Abstracts
##### **Discriminative World Models for Web Agents**
2609.02885v1 by Kelvin Li, Dhruv Pendharkar, Anish Pahilajani, Chuyi Shang, Leon Oks, Leonid Karlinsky, Rogerio Feris, Trevor Darrell, Roei Herzig

Recent web agents use world models for test-time action selection by sampling candidate actions, predicting the resulting web states, and ranking them with a ranker model or a Process Reward Model (PRM). These world models are typically trained via supervised next-state prediction to generate fixed representations like HTML or AXTree snapshots. However, this objective is misaligned with the downstream ranker, which relies on predicted states being discriminative across candidates to accurately score them. To address this, we introduce predicted-state matching, a training objective where the predicted representation must distinguish the true resulting state from those reached by alternative actions. We train these models using a branching web-agent dataset derived from WebArena Go-Browse trajectories, where every decision point contains multiple alternative actions and their resulting states. Experiments on our held-out predicted-state matching benchmark show that our approach outperforms world models trained with supervised next-state prediction. We further show that our approach improves PRM-style action ranking on WebPRMBench compared with action-only PRMs and PRMs augmented with supervised-next-state world models. Finally, on WebArena-Lite, using our world model for test-time action selection improves end-to-end task success. Our project page is available at: https://dhruvpendharkar.github.io/dwm/.

摘要：最近的網路代理使用世界模型進行測試時的行動選擇，通過抽樣候選行動、預測結果網路狀態，並使用排名模型或過程獎勵模型（PRM）對其進行排名。這些世界模型通常通過監督式的下一狀態預測進行訓練，以生成固定的表示，如HTML或AXTree快照。然而，這一目標與下游排名器不一致，因為下游排名器依賴於預測狀態在候選之間具有區分性，以準確評分。為了解決這個問題，我們引入了預測狀態匹配，這是一個訓練目標，其中預測的表示必須區分真實的結果狀態與其他行動所達到的狀態。我們使用從WebArena Go-Browse軌跡衍生的分支網路代理數據集來訓練這些模型，其中每個決策點包含多個替代行動及其結果狀態。在我們的保留預測狀態匹配基準上的實驗顯示，我們的方法優於使用監督式下一狀態預測訓練的世界模型。我們進一步顯示，與僅行動的PRM和增強了監督式下一狀態世界模型的PRM相比，我們的方法在WebPRMBench上改善了PRM風格的行動排名。最後，在WebArena-Lite上，使用我們的世界模型進行測試時的行動選擇改善了端到端任務成功率。我們的項目頁面可在以下網址找到：https://dhruvpendharkar.github.io/dwm/.

##### **Towards Trustworthy Autonomous Robots: An Explainable AI-Based Decision Framework**
2609.02861v1 by Cagri Temel

Autonomous robots powered by deep learning face a fundamental auditability challenge: when incidents occur, investigators cannot reconstruct why the system made specific decisions. This paper presents TRACE (Transparent Reasoning Architecture for Credible Execution), a decision framework that ensures every autonomous action can be traced back to sensor evidence through documented causal chains. We evaluate the framework using three objective metrics: Evidence Traceability (sensor-to-decision linkage), Decision Reconstructability (post-hoc analysis capability), and Temporal Continuity (audit trail completeness). Experimental evaluation on warehouse robot navigation demonstrates that TRACE achieves 98.6% evidence traceability, 99.0% temporal continuity, and 98.1% decision reconstructability across 500 simulated decision cycles. The framework addresses EU AI Act requirements for high-risk system transparency and contributes to Explainable AI for safety-critical autonomous systems.

In this work, we propose a new TSRL framework, namely multi-source multi-phase time series representation transfer (SMart), which has two novel mechanisms to address the aforementioned deficiencies: 1) a multi-phase recurrence plots recovery task, in three alternative modes, for guiding the encoder to embed time series dynamics into the time series representation; and 2) a source dataset selector to select multiple suitable source datasets to supplement the original target dataset for pre-training the TSRL encoder. Experimental results show that SMart outperforms several state-of-the-art models for time series representation learning, classification and regression on both uni-variate and multi-variate time series datasets, reducing mean absolute error up to 19.5% for time series regression, and increasing average accuracy up to 1.34\% for time series classification.

摘要：時間序列表示學習（TSRL）在近年來引起了越來越多的研究興趣。最近在TSRL中的兩個探索是：i) 利用基於Transformer的框架來學習時間序列；ii) 不僅使用目標數據集，而是借用其他數據集的時間序列來促進表示轉移。雖然這兩個探索被證明是有效的，但（i）中的自我監督時間序列恢復任務和（ii）中使用的單一來源數據集在技術上是簡單的，因此可以通過新想法進行增強。在這項工作中，我們提出了一個新的TSRL框架，即多來源多階段時間序列表示轉移（SMart），它有兩個新機制來解決上述缺陷：1) 一個多階段回歸圖恢復任務，以三種替代模式引導編碼器將時間序列動態嵌入時間序列表示中；2) 一個來源數據集選擇器，用於選擇多個合適的來源數據集，以補充原始目標數據集，用於預訓練TSRL編碼器。實驗結果顯示，SMart在時間序列表示學習、分類和回歸方面超越了幾個最先進的模型，對於單變量和多變量時間序列數據集，將時間序列回歸的平均絕對誤差降低了高達19.5%，並將時間序列分類的平均準確率提高了高達1.34%。
